<details>
<summary><strong>提示词</strong> — Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect ratio: 16:9 | Style: Cinematic gritty thriller action SCENE Dilapidated industrial warehouse. Rusty orange metal shelvin…</summary>

~~~~text
Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect ratio: 16:9 | Style: Cinematic gritty thriller action

SCENE
Dilapidated industrial warehouse. Rusty orange metal shelving units, hanging heavy iron chains, concrete floor covered in shattered glass debris. A woman with dark hair tied back, wearing a white button-down shirt heavily stained with fresh blood over the chest, holding a jagged glass shard. A man with dark hair, wearing a distressed, torn black denim jacket over a black shirt.

SHOT BREAKDOWN
0-4s — Wide profile two-shot framed through foreground blurred glass. The woman holds the glass shard defensively, staring down the man across a dusty aisle.
4-8s — Medium action shot. The woman lunges forward aggressively, swinging the glass shard. The man swiftly reacts, intercepting her arm mid-strike.
8-12s — Medium close-up. The man forcefully drives the woman back, pinning her against the rusty metal shelving. Her arm is trapped, neutralizing the weapon.
12-15s — Alternating over-the-shoulder close-ups. First, the woman’s bruised face, blood dripping prominently from her lower lip, lips moving in quiet defiance. Cut to the man’s face, jaw clenched, eyes narrowed with intense anger as he grips her collar.

CAMERA
Handheld dynamic motion, snapping into rapid focus during the struggle. Shallow depth of field isolating faces in close-ups. Fast, kinetic cut rhythm.

LIGHTING & PALETTE
Single overhead fluorescent tube casts harsh, directional top-light. Deep shadows filling the background. Cool, sickly cyan and green color grade sharply contrasting with vivid crimson blood.

AUDIO
Industrial hollow room tone, crunching broken glass, sudden fabric rustling, violent scuffle impacts. Dark, pulsing synth drone. Tense, breathless dialogue.

AVOID
Bright key lighting, clean pristine environments, static tripod shots, cheerful atmosphere, slow motion.
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
SCENE
An urban night setting featuring a young man with a white cheek bandage, oversized black jacket, and light cream trousers on a white cafe racer motorcycle. A young woman with blunt black bangs, a sheer cropped top, and shiny black mini-skirt joins him outside a illuminated storefront before riding through a highway tunnel.

SHOT BREAKDOWN
0-4s — Medium wide shot outside a fluorescent-lit storefront; the man straddles his motorcycle lighting a cigarette as the woman steps out the glass door and gracefully mounts the back seat.
4-8s — Medium tracking shot inside a subterranean expressway tunnel; the woman leans forward, resting her chin and arm affectionately on his shoulder while her dark hair blows backward in the wind.
8-12s — Close-up side tracking profile; the man steers calmly with a glowing cigarette between his lips while the woman peacefully closes her eyes against his jacket, cyan ceiling lights flashing rapidly overhead.
12-15s — Low-angle perspective from behind the motorcycle as it accelerates down the center line of the roadway, roaring toward the tunnel's vanishing point under repeating illuminated overhead fixtures.

CAMERA
Cuts from a static street-level medium shot to fluid side-tracking profiles matching the motorcycle's velocity, concluding on a low-angle trailing shot with deep perspective.

LIGHTING & PALETTE
High-contrast nocturnal aesthetics dominated by deep navy shadows, bright warm convenience store glows, and intense teal and cyan tunnel strip lights casting dramatic edge highlights.

AUDIO
Subdued ambient city murmur transitioning into a guttural, throaty four-stroke motorcycle engine revving, rushing atmospheric wind effects, and a moody electronic synthwave beat.

AVOID
Photorealism, live-action footage, soft gradient shading, anatomical warping, background motion blur, visible pedestrians.
~~~~

<details>
<summary><strong>提示词</strong> — Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect ratio: 16:9 | Style: High-end cosmetics commercial, macro beauty photography, elegant cinematic realism SCENE Matte blac…</summary>

~~~~text
Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect ratio: 16:9 | Style: High-end cosmetics commercial, macro beauty photography, elegant cinematic realism

SCENE
Matte black lipstick tube with rose-gold accents. Vibrant matte red lipstick. A fair-skinned woman with dark hair in a black strapless dress. Abstract environments featuring glossy black podiums, floating red rose petals, sparkling diamonds, and undulating glossy red silk.

SHOT BREAKDOWN
0-4s — Extreme close-up pans across the closed black tube resting on a sparkling red surface. Text: "UNVEIL TRUE LUXURY". Cuts to the tube standing upright as a sparkling red liquid ribbon swirls around it among falling rose petals. Text: "INTENSE VELVET COLOR" and "SMOOTH • WEIGHTLESS • PRECISION".
4-8s — The tube opens to reveal the red lipstick; a golden light trail traces the tip amid floating petals and diamonds. Cuts to a profile shot of a woman applying the lipstick. Text: "ONE GLIDE." and "PERFECT COVERAGE."
8-12s — The woman looks through a lighted vanity mirror frame. Text: "RICH PIGMENT • LASTING ELEGANCE." Cuts to her smiling over her shoulder with flowing hair and floating red silk in the background. Text: "CRAFTED FOR" and "CONFIDENCE".
12-15s — Extreme macro close-up of perfectly painted bright red lips. The final shot frames the open lipstick on a glossy podium under warm volumetric god rays, surrounded by floating silk and diamonds. Text: "YOUR BRAND NAME" and "Own Every Moment".

CAMERA
Macro lenses with shallow depth of field. Smooth tracking pans, slow-motion dynamics for floating elements, and sharp, rhythmic commercial cuts.

LIGHTING & PALETTE
High-contrast studio lighting, luminous vanity strip lights, and golden god rays. Color palette dominated by pitch blacks, vivid velvet reds, sparkling whites, and warm metallic golds.

AUDIO
~~~~

~~~~text
AUDIO
~~~~

~~~~text
AVOID
Handheld camera shake, harsh shadows, cluttered backgrounds, unnatural eye tracking, dull skin tones.
~~~~

~~~~text
AUDIO
~~~~

### 144. 废墟食尸鬼袭击 `AI 反推`

<a href="https://tryminimax.asia/minimax-h3-prompts#prompt-cmsb5cenv000504l1qr735c3x"><img src="https://pbs.twimg.com/amplify_video_thumb/2082493201869348864/img/1ON-XOrowJjDPQ26.jpg" alt="废墟食尸鬼袭击" width="700" /></a>

<details>
<summary><strong>提示词</strong> — Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect ratio: 16:9 | Style: Found footage horror, VHS glitch aesthetic SCENE Young Asian woman wearing a bright yellow tank top…</summary>

~~~~text
Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect ratio: 16:9 | Style: Found footage horror, VHS glitch aesthetic

SCENE
Young Asian woman wearing a bright yellow tank top and frayed denim shorts. Emaciated ghoul with sickly pale skin, elongated limbs, sharp fangs, and bony spikes protruding from its spine. Dilapidated, pitch-black room featuring peeling green wall paint, dirt, and scattered wooden debris.

SHOT BREAKDOWN
0-4s — The ghoul violently chokes the terrified woman against a deteriorating wall. She struggles desperately, her hands gripping its elongated gray arms to relieve the pressure. Framed as a medium two-shot.
4-8s — The creature unhinges its jaw, roaring directly into her face. The sheer force of the roar blows the woman's dark hair wildly backward as she screams in agony. Camera pushes in for a tight close-up.
8-12s — The brutal struggle continues. The woman frantically tries to pry the monster's unyielding grip from her throat, her expression locked in pure terror.
12-15s — Sudden harsh VHS tracking glitches distort the frame. The ghoul unexpectedly disengages, sitting back on its haunches to flash a sinister, bloody grin directly at the camera lens. The woman collapses in the background, sobbing.

CAMERA
Handheld, erratic, and shaky, simulating an amateur camcorder. Heavy use of direct flash perspective. Aggressive VHS tracking lines and analog distortion tear through the final seconds.

LIGHTING & PALETTE
Harsh, direct frontal flash lighting creating stark, pitch-black background shadows. Color palette dominated by sickly corpse grays, stark neon yellow, faded denim blue, and murky browns.

AUDIO
Shrill, panicked female screaming, wet guttural monster roaring, frantic physical struggling. Piercing tape static and audio tearing during the final glitch sequence.

AVOID
Smooth camera stabilization, professional three-point lighting, cheerful atmosphere, modern clean lenses, shallow depth of field.
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
4-8s — She shifts her posture back, smiling brightly and pointing toward her collar while singing, framed in a steady medium shot.
8-12s — She crosses her forearms across her chest, then smoothly sweeps her hands outward in a synchronized dance movement.
12-15s — She sings enthusiastically with a wide expression, lifting her hands outward and bouncing lightly to the beat.

CAMERA
Eye-level medium-full shot with a smartphone wide-angle lens feel. Subtle, natural handheld shake maintains center framing on the subject with zero cuts.

LIGHTING & PALETTE
Soft overcast daytime lighting with subtle urban reflections from surrounding glass. Color palette emphasizes black, maroon, silver, and cool gray tones.

AUDIO
Upbeat urban hip-hop music with a steady bassline. The woman's lips clearly sync to lively, rhythmic pop-rap vocals.

AVOID
Morphing limbs, unstable facial features, camera cuts, overexposed lighting, shifting wardrobe details.
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect ratio: 16:9 | Style: First-person mixed-media magical realism blending live-action with glowing neon 2D animations.

SCENE
Unseen protagonist's hands, brown jacket sleeve. Retro laundromat with metallic washing machines, beige tiles, fluorescent lights, wooden bench, white laundry basket. Floating neon line-art animations: jellyfish, sock, fish, toast, truck, geometric energy.

SHOT BREAKDOWN
0-4s — Fingers pinch a fuzzy, glowing purple orb. Cut to first-person view looking down into a laundry basket where a neon purple line-art jellyfish floats above white garments.
4-8s — Low angle pan to a glowing neon sock hovering beside a real grey sock on the floor. Cut to first-person hand reaching toward a washing machine door; a neon cyan fish swims on the glass.
8-12s — Hand opens the washing machine. Neon purple toast and a white truck float out amid drawn smoke. Cut to hand holding a glowing purple geometric cube near a blue vending machine.

LIGHTING & PALETTE
Harsh overhead fluorescent practical lighting. Physical space features muted beige, silver, and white. Animated elements emit intense luminescent neon purple, cyan, and pink. Final scene is deep space-black with vibrant neon outlines.

AUDIO
Humming fluorescent lights, tumbling washing machines, synthetic magical chimes, electronic synth beat.

AVOID
Third-person perspective, photorealistic CGI entities, natural daylight, static framing, deep depth of field.
~~~~

<details>
<summary><strong>提示词</strong> — Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect ratio: 16:9 | Style: Handheld action sports parkour videography SCENE Athletic young man, short black hair, black t-shir…</summary>

~~~~text
Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect ratio: 16:9 | Style: Handheld action sports parkour videography

SCENE
Athletic young man, short black hair, black t-shirt, olive green cargo pants, white sneakers. Urban environment transitioning from narrow concrete alleyways and tiled stairwells to high-rise commercial rooftops with metal railings and HVAC ventilation units.

SHOT BREAKDOWN
0-4s — Man executes a parkour vault over a metal barricade fence in a narrow alley, followed by a dynamic leap over a concrete parapet against a clear blue sky. Framed from low tracking angles.
4-8s — Man jumps directly toward the camera down an outdoor flight of tiled stairs. Cuts to a high-angle view as he drops from a higher ledge toward a lower rooftop featuring industrial air conditioning units.
8-12s — Man vaults smoothly over a thick yellow metal railing. Cuts to an overhead perspective tracking him as he scales down a concrete wall to another roof tier.
12-15s — Man lands in a balanced crouch on the edge of a roof parapet. He executes a parkour safety roll onto the flat gray rooftop surface. The camera pulls back to a high aerial wide shot revealing a sprawling metropolitan skyline with skyscrapers.

CAMERA
Wide-angle action lens. Handheld, energetic tracking movements following the subject's momentum. Rapid cuts matching the physical impact of the jumps. Extreme low and high angles emphasize verticality and height.

LIGHTING & PALETTE
Bright midday sunlight, harsh direct lighting causing sharp, high-contrast shadows. Dominant colors include neutral concrete grays, muted olive greens, bright flat yellow, and clear sky blue.

AUDIO
Whooshing wind noise, heavy landing thuds, rapid footsteps, sneaker squeaks, clothing rustle, distant city traffic drone. Fast-paced electronic action instrumental.

AVOID
Slow motion, static tripod shots, nighttime scenes, indoor settings, formal clothing, stylized neon lighting.
~~~~

</details>

<a href="https://tryminimax.asia/minimax-h3-prompts#prompt-cmsb4zuua000f04l12f3kcyfj"><img src="https://pbs.twimg.com/amplify_video_thumb/2082869570709417984/img/BhZa3fohdWz8mtzd.jpg" alt="跨越世界的无缝穿梭" width="700" /></a>

<details>
<summary><strong>提示词</strong> — Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect ratio: 16:9 | Style: Cinematic surreal environment transition montage SCENE Young woman, dark wavy hair, white satin dre…</summary>

~~~~text
Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect ratio: 16:9 | Style: Cinematic surreal environment transition montage

SCENE
Young woman, dark wavy hair, white satin dress with a high slit, camel blazer draped over shoulders, emerald and diamond necklace. She travels seamlessly through rapidly changing fantastical environments.

SHOT BREAKDOWN
0-4s — Woman walks confidently through a rainy cyberpunk city with glowing neon billboards and flying vehicles, transitioning instantly to a traditional Japanese temple with falling pink cherry blossoms.
4-8s — The environment shifts to an underwater realm featuring large sharks, glowing jellyfish, and massive crystal formations. The scene cuts to a gray lunar base with astronauts; the woman turns away, facing a giant Earth in the sky.
8-12s — Seen from a profile angle, she stands in a foggy mystical forest surrounded by giant bioluminescent mushrooms. The backdrop abruptly changes to a dark volcanic landscape with actively erupting lava and smoke.
12-15s — She faces forward inside a modern minimalist penthouse with floor-to-ceiling windows overlooking a sea of clouds at sunset. The final cut places her in deep space against a brightly lit spiral galaxy and stars.

CAMERA
Locked-on tracking shot, maintaining medium-full framing. Rapid, seamless smash cuts between backgrounds while the subject's position remains relatively stationary.

LIGHTING & PALETTE
Highly dynamic and contrasting. Neon magentas and cyans, warm golden hour tones, deep aquatic blues, fiery orange lava, and vivid cosmic purples. 

AUDIO
Driving electronic beat. Heavy swooshing transition sound effects synchronized with the rapid environmental cuts. No dialogue.

AVOID
Wardrobe changes, character morphing, camera shake, shallow depth of field obscuring backgrounds, muted colors.
~~~~

</details>

Backgrounds alternate between concrete brutalism, glowing metallic tunnels, and minimalist photo studios. Giant text overlays integrate with the environment.

~~~~text
AUDIO

AVOID
Soft focus, warm natural sunlight, organic environments, slow crossfades, shaky handheld camera.
~~~~

~~~~text
Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect ratio: 16:9 | Style: Cinematic drama, emotional cross-cutting dialogue

SCENE
A distressed woman with an updo wearing a beige turtleneck sweater and holding a dark coat in an airport terminal gate at night. A somber man in a grey t-shirt inside a dimly lit kitchen with rain-speckled windows.

SHOT BREAKDOWN
0-4s — Medium shot of the woman standing among rows of empty blue airport seats, speaking anxiously on her phone.
4-8s — Cut to a medium shot of the man leaning against a kitchen counter, listening solemnly on his phone.
8-12s — Cut to a tight close-up of the woman, teary-eyed and speaking with deep emotion.
12-15s — Cut to a tight close-up of the man closing his eyes with a pained, heartbroken expression.

CAMERA
50mm and 85mm prime lens feel, shallow depth of field, static framing, eye-level medium shots cutting cleanly to intimate close-ups.

LIGHTING & PALETTE
Airport setting uses cool white overhead lighting, dark glass reflections, deep blues, and beige. Kitchen setting features warm pendant lighting, heavy shadows, and amber outdoor bokeh.

AUDIO
Low airport terminal hum, soft ambient drone, distant rain against window glass, quiet emotional phone dialogue.

AVOID
Camera shake, daylight, fast whip pans, exaggerated body motion, upbeat background music.
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
Duration: 15 seconds | Aspect ratio: 16:9 | Style: Realistic everyday vlog video

SCENE
Three young Asian women wearing casual summer clothes including tank tops, t-shirts, and denim shorts, standing outside a suburban house. A large grey monitor lizard clinging to a black metal driveway gate, and a residential street with houses.

SHOT BREAKDOWN
0-4s — Medium shot of three women standing by an open residential gate, laughing and reacting as a large lizard climbs the metal bars. One woman records the animal on a smartphone while a motorcycle handlebar and rearview mirror frame the foreground.
4-8s — Wide shot of a sunny suburban street as a delivery rider wearing a turquoise helmet drives a scooter toward the camera, carrying a cardboard parcel and a square delivery box.
8-12s — Medium side-profile shot of the three women watching the lizard on the gate. The woman in the green shirt holds her smartphone close to film the reptile as it clings to the vertical bars.
12-15s — Low-angle shot looking down at the women's bare legs and feet wearing sandals and sneakers on the bright concrete driveway near the black metal gate.

CAMERA
Handheld digital camera with natural depth of field. Cuts between eye-level medium framing, a wide street view, and a downward low-angle perspective.

LIGHTING & PALETTE
Bright midday outdoor sunlight casting crisp shadows on concrete. Naturalistic colour palette featuring foliage greens, neutral greys, white, and matte black metal.

AUDIO
Ambient outdoor street atmosphere, faint scooter engine hum, and casual background laughter from the women.

AVOID
Unrealistic CGI effects, studio lighting, cinematic color grading, distorted anatomy, camera shake, on-screen text overlays.
~~~~
